## 📎 Attachment · skill_listing · 2026-08-13T05:40:18.494Z

```
- dataviz: Use this skill whenever you are about to create ANY chart, graph, plot, dashboard, or data visualization, in ANY output medium — an HTML or React artifact, inline SVG, plotting code in any library (matplotlib, plotly, d3, Recharts, …), an image/PNG you will render and upload, or a chart shared into Slack. Read it BEFORE writing the first line of chart code, choosing chart colors, building a stat tile / meter / KPI row, or laying out a dashboard. Produces visualizations that read as one system — elegant, accessible, consistent in light and dark — using a brand-neutral placeholder palette you swap for your own. Teaches a design-system-agnostic method: a form heuristic, a color formula with a runnable validator, mark specs, and interaction rules. A validated default palette is documented in `references/palette.md` — swap that file's values for your brand's. Triggers on: "chart", "graph", "plot", "data viz", "visualization", "dashboard", "analytics", "visualize data", "categorical colors", "sequential / diverging palette", "stat tile", "sparkline", "heatmap", "legend", "axis", "tooltip", "chart colors", "color by series".
- update-config: Use this skill to configure the Claude Code harness via settings.json. Automated behaviors ("from now on when X", "each time X", "whenever X", "before/after X") require hooks configured in settings.json - the harness executes these, not Claude, so memory/preferences cannot fulfill them. Also use for: permissions ("allow X", "add permission", "move permission to"), env vars ("set X=Y"), hook troubleshooting, or any changes to settings.json/settings.local.json files. Examples: "allow npm commands", "add bq permission to global settings", "move permission to user settings", "set DEBUG=true", "when claude stops show X". For simple settings like theme/model, suggest the /config command.
- keybindings-help: Use when the user wants to customize keyboard shortcuts, rebind keys, add chord bindings, or modify ~/.claude/keybindings.json. Examples: "rebind ctrl+s", "add a chord shortcut", "change the submit key", "customize keybindings".
- simplify: Review the changed code for reuse, simplification, efficiency, and altitude cleanups, then apply the fixes. Quality only — it does not hunt for bugs; use /code-review for that.
- fewer-permission-prompts: Scan your transcripts for common read-only Bash and MCP tool calls, then add a prioritized allowlist to project .claude/settings.json to reduce permission prompts.
- loop: Run a prompt or slash command on a recurring interval (e.g. /loop 5m /foo, defaults to 10m) - When the user wants to set up a recurring task, poll for status, or run something repeatedly on an interval (e.g. "check the deploy every 5 minutes", "keep running /babysit-prs"). Do NOT invoke for one-off tasks.
- claude-api: Reference for the Claude API / Anthropic SDK — model ids, pricing, params, streaming, tool use, MCP, agents, caching, token counting, model migration.
TRIGGER — read BEFORE opening the target file; don't skip because it "looks like a one-liner" — whenever: the prompt names Claude/Anthropic in any form (Claude, Anthropic, Fable, Opus, Sonnet, Haiku, `anthropic`, `@anthropic-ai`, `claude-*`, `us.anthropic.*`, `[1m]`); the user asks about an LLM (pricing/model choice/limits/caching) — never answer from memory; OR the task is LLM-shaped with provider unstated (agent/MCP/tool-definition/multi-agent/RAG/LLM-judge/computer-use; generate/summarize/extract/classify/rewrite/converse over NL; debugging refusals/cutoffs/streaming/tool-calls/tokens).
SKIP only when another provider is being worked on (overrides all triggers): OpenAI/GPT/Gemini/Llama/Mistral/Cohere/Ollama named in the query; OR `grep -rE 'openai|langchain_openai|google.generativeai|genai|mistralai|cohere|ollama'` over the project hits (run this grep FIRST if no provider named — don't Read the file).
- run: Launch and drive this project's app to see a change working. Use when asked to run, start, or screenshot the app, or to confirm a change works in the real app (not just tests). First looks for a project skill that already covers launching the app; otherwise falls back to built-in patterns per project type (CLI, server, TUI, Electron, browser-driven, library).
- init: Initialize a new CLAUDE.md file with codebase documentation
- review: Review a GitHub pull request; for your working diff use /code-review
- security-review: Complete a security review of the pending changes on the current branch
```

```
133	const BACKFILL_WALLS = new Set([0, 54, 55, 56, 57, 58, 59, 61, 185, 212, 213, 214, 215, 2, 196, 197, 198, 199, 15, 40, 71, 64, 204, 205, 206, 207, 208, 209, 210, 211]);

135	/** initializeHardMode（cs:31734-31860）1:1（remix/dualDungeons/skyblock 分支不适用） */
136	export function initializeHardMode(world: World, rng: RNG): void {
137	  const st = world.store, w = st.w, h = st.h;
138	  // ---- V 带位置（cs:31740-31781）----
139	  const num = rng.nextIntRange(300, 400) * 0.001;        // 神圣带侧比例
140	  const num2 = rng.nextIntRange(200, 300) * 0.001;       // 邪恶带侧比例
141	  let num3 = Math.trunc(w * num);                        // 神圣带 x
142	  let num4 = Math.trunc(w * (1 - num));                  // 邪恶带 x
143	  let num5 = 1;
144	  if (rng.nextInt(2) === 0) {
145	    num4 = Math.trunc(w * num);
146	    num3 = Math.trunc(w * (1 - num));
147	    num5 = -1;
148	  }
149	  const num6 = world.dungeonX < w / 2 ? -1 : 1;          // 神圣带放地牢对侧
150	  if (num6 < 0) {
151	    if (num4 < num3) num4 = Math.trunc(w * num2);
152	    else num3 = Math.trunc(w * num2);
153	  } else if (num4 > num3) num4 = Math.trunc(w * (1 - num2));
154	  else num3 = Math.trunc(w * (1 - num2));
155	  geRunner(st, rng, num3, 0, 3 * num5, 5.0, true, world.crimson);
156	  geRunner(st, rng, num4, 0, -3 * num5, 5.0, false, world.crimson);
157	  // ---- 洞穴墙回填（cs:31783-31860）：25×(w/4200) 片 ----
158	  let budget = Math.trunc(25 * (w / 4200));
159	  let attempts = 0;
160	  while (budget > 0) {
161	    if (++attempts % 15000 === 0) budget--;              // 原版放弃退减（防死循环）
162	    // RandomWorldPoint(worldSurface-100, 1, 190, 1)：x∈[1,w-2]、y∈[ws-100,h-191]
163	    const px = rng.nextIntRange(1, w - 1);
164	    const py = rng.nextIntRange(Math.max(20, Math.trunc(world.groundLevel) - 100), h - 190);
165	    if (!st.inBounds(px, py)) continue;
166	    const ii = st.idx(px, py);
167	    const sh = TILE_DEFS[st.type[ii]]?.vanilla?.sheet ?? -1;
168	    let wallId = 0;
169	    if (SET_CRIMSON.has(sh)) wallId = 192 + rng.nextInt(4);       // 猩红墙 192-195
170	    else if (SET_CORRUPT.has(sh)) wallId = 188 + rng.nextInt(4);  // 腐化墙 188-191
171	    else if (SET_HALLOW.has(sh)) wallId = 200 + rng.nextInt(4);   // 神圣墙 200-203
172	    if (st.flags[ii] && wallId !== 0 && !st.flags[st.idx(px, py - 1)]) {
173	      // ShapeFloodFill(1000)：非实心 + OnlyWalls 白名单泛洪；OuterOutline(+对角+内腔)
174	      const cells = new Set<number>();
175	      let complete = true;
176	      const queue: Array<[number, number]> = [[px, py - 1]];
177	      let quota = 1000;
178	      while (queue.length && quota > 0) {
179	        const [qx, qy] = queue.shift()!;
180	        const qi = st.idx(qx, qy);
181	        if (cells.has(qi)) continue;
182	        if (st.flags[qi]) continue;                      // IsNotSolid
183	        if (!BACKFILL_WALLS.has(st.wall[qi])) continue;  // OnlyWalls
184	        cells.add(qi);
185	        quota--;
186	        if (qx + 1 < w - 1) queue.push([qx + 1, qy]);
187	        if (qx - 1 >= 1) queue.push([qx - 1, qy]);
188	        if (qy + 1 < h - 1) queue.push([qx, qy + 1]);
189	        if (qy - 1 >= 1) queue.push([qx, qy - 1]);
190	      }
191	      if (quota <= 0 && queue.length) complete = false;
192	      if (cells.size > 50 && complete) {
193	        for (const ci of cells) {                        // 腔体 + 8 邻环铺墙
194	          const cx = ci % st.w, cy = Math.trunc(ci / st.w);
195	          const paint = (wx: number, wy: number): void => {
196	            if (st.inBounds(wx, wy)) st.setWall(wx, wy, wallId);
197	          };
198	          paint(cx + 1, cy); paint(cx - 1, cy); paint(cx, cy + 1); paint(cx, cy - 1);
199	          paint(cx + 1, cy + 1); paint(cx + 1, cy - 1); paint(cx - 1, cy + 1); paint(cx - 1, cy - 1);
200	          paint(cx, cy);
201	        }
202	        budget--;
203	      }
204	    }
205	  }
206	}
207	
208	/** CreateBrickBoxForWallOfFlesh（NPC.cs:79837-79866）：击杀点空心砖盒+清岩浆。
209	 *  cxTile/cyTile = 击杀 NPC 中心格；widthPx = 碰撞盒宽。盒半径 = (int)(width/2/16)+1
210	 *  （C# 整除链：100/2=50 → 50/16=3 → +1=4） */
211	export function createBrickBoxForWallOfFlesh(st: TileStore, cxTile: number, cyTile: number, widthPx: number, crimson: boolean): void {
212	  const r = Math.trunc(widthPx / 2 / 16) + 1;
213	  const brick = crimson ? id(347) : id(140);             // 猩红砖 347 / 魔金砖 140
214	  for (let i = cxTile - r; i <= cxTile + r; i++) {
215	    for (let j = cyTile - r; j <= cyTile + r; j++) {
216	      if (!st.inBounds(i, j)) continue;
217	      const ii = st.idx(i, j);
218	      const onShell = i === cxTile - r || i === cxTile + r || j === cyTile - r || j === cyTile + r;
219	      if (onShell && !st.flags[ii]) {
220	        st.setTile(i, j, brick);
221	      }
222	      st.liquid[ii] = 0; st.liquidType[ii] = 0;          // 盒内岩浆清零
223	    }
224	  }
225	}
226	
227	/** StartHardmode（cs:31866-31880）+ SetEventFlagCleared（NPC.cs:80281-80292）语义。
228	 *  调用方（Game 击杀流）按原版顺序执行：brickbox → 捕获旧 hardMode → 本函数 →
229	 *  (!wasHard 时) 灯笼夜 19 → misc[15] 公告 + 成就 9（由返回值委托，调用方消费）。 */
230	export function startHardmode(world: World): { wasHard: boolean } {
231	  const wasHard = !!world.flags.hardMode;
232	  (world.flags as Record<string, boolean>).hardMode = true;
233	  initializeHardMode(world, new RNG(world.seed));
234	  return { wasHard };
235	}
```

```
8	 *  （frameX==18 && frameY==0——3×2 物件的顶行中列），向上/向下 50 格找实心，
9	 *  落位 y = (上 + 上 + 下)/3（向上偏置），x = 祭坛列中心。
10	 *  返回 null = ±20 格内无祭坛（:81308-81311 静默返回）。
11	 *  @param altarTileId 内部 tile id（TILE_BY_KEY['v_237_lihzahrd_altar']） */
12	export function golemSpawnFromAltar(
13	  st: TileStore, altarTileId: number, pcx: number, pcy: number,
14	): { x: number; y: number; altarX: number; altarY: number } | null {
15	  let ax = 0, ay = 0;
16	  for (let j = Math.max(0, pcx - 20); j < Math.min(st.w, pcx + 20); j++) {
17	    for (let k = Math.max(0, pcy - 20); k < Math.min(st.h, pcy + 20); k++) {
18	      const i = st.idx(j, k);
19	      if (st.type[i] !== altarTileId) continue;
20	      if (st.frameX[i] === 18 && st.frameY[i] === 0) { ax = j; ay = k; }   // :81300-81304
21	    }
22	  }
23	  if (ax <= 0 || ay <= 0) return null;
24	  let up = ay - 15, down = ay - 15;     // num6/num7 初值 = num5-15（:81313-81314）
25	  for (let y = ay; y > ay - 50; y--) {  // 向上 50 找实心（:81315-81322）
26	    if (st.inBounds(ax, y) && st.isSolid(ax, y)) { up = y; break; }
27	  }
28	  for (let y = ay; y < ay + 50; y++) {  // 向下 50 找实心（:81323-81330 前段）
29	    if (st.inBounds(ax, y) && st.isSolid(ax, y)) { down = y; break; }
30	  }
31	  const gy = Math.floor((up + up + down) / 3);   // :81324（向上 2:1 偏置）
32	  return { x: ax * 16 + 8, y: gy * 16, altarX: ax, altarY: ay };
33	}
34

37	export function eterniaStandBottomRow(frameY: number): number {
38	  if (frameY === 0) return 3;
39	  if (frameY === 18) return 2;
40	  if (frameY === 36) return 1;
41	  return 0;
42	}
```

```
4	//     地下 = w×h×1.5e-5 样本走 UpdateWorld_UndergroundTile（:73266）
5	//     每地表样本 1/(num7×100) 概率 PlantAlch 药草自播种（:45910，num7=151..422.8 随世界宽）
6	//   UpdateWorld_GrassGrowth（:74333）：草/丛林草/蘑菇草/灰烬草/邪恶草蔓延 + 顶部植物
7	//   hardUpdateWorld（:69661，困难模式）：邪恶/神圣草蔓延 + 水晶碎块(129) + 叶绿(211) + 邪恶矿退化
8	//   藤蔓各族生长（GrowMoreVines 密度门 :45592）+ 仙人掌（:56584）+ 染料植物（:71298）
9	//   苔藓蔓延（MossConversion :71469）+ 藓堆(184) + 钟乳石再生（PlaceTight :38329）
10	//   蛛网再生（GrowWeb :74141）+ 贝壳/珊瑚沉积 + 药草三态（GrowAlch :45786）
11	//   墙蔓延（SpreadGrassWalls :74958 / SpreadDesertWalls :75073）→ Convert 墙半区（:55113）
12	//   SpreadGrass 运行时版（:75210）：普通草仅地表（j<worldSurface 且 dirt==0）、邪恶草不受限、
13	//   3×3 封死不转、邻岩浆不转、上方向日葵(27)挡邪恶草、草→草走 Convert tiles-only
14	// 近似项（原版依赖系统缺失，注释标明）：
15	//   - Collision.CanHitLine（GrowMoreVines 的加权计数）→ 仅计数不判视线
16	//   - PlayerLOS（球茎/蜂巢/蚁狮幼虫隐藏性）→ 玩家距离 >50 格视为不可见
17	//   - 装饰变体帧（PlaceTile 后 TileFrame 随机化）→ 直接随机变体帧
18	//   - 环境转换器 Convert 其余 case（5/6/7/10/12/13）不在演化路径，未移植；
19	//     case 11 净化已由净化粉弹体链补齐（见 convert()，Projectile.cs:22494 消费方）
20	import type { TileStore } from '../TileStore';
21	import { TILE_DEFS } from '../../data/tiles';
22	import { NOT_CLEARABLE } from '../gen/vanilla/TileRunner';
23	
24	/** sheet id → 内部 tile id（首匹配；TILE_DEFS 顺序固定，模块加载时一次构建） */
25	const SID = new Map<number, number>();
26	for (let id = 0; id < TILE_DEFS.length; id++) {
27	  const sh = TILE_DEFS[id]?.vanilla?.sheet;
28	  if (sh != null && !SID.has(sh)) SID.set(sh, id);
29	}
30	const S = (sheet: number): number => SID.get(sheet) ?? 0;
31	
32	// ---- 原版集合表（TileID.cs / WallID.cs / Main.cs 实证）----
33	// TileID.Sets.SpreadOverground（TileID.cs:413）
34	const SPREAD_OVERGROUND = new Set([2, 23, 661, 32, 60, 70, 109, 199, 662, 352, 477, 492, 633, 226]);
35	// TileID.Sets.SpreadUnderground（:415）
36	const SPREAD_UNDERGROUND = new Set([23, 661, 109, 199, 662, 60, 70, 633, 226]);
37	// TileID.Sets.IsVine（:237）
38	const IS_VINE = new Set([52, 382, 62, 115, 205, 528, 636, 638]);
39	// TileID.Sets.CanGrowCrystalShards（:341）
40	const CAN_GROW_SHARDS = new Set([117, 116, 164, 402, 403]);
41	// TileID.Sets.Spreads{Corruption,Crimson,Hallow}（:331/:349/:339）
42	const SPREADS_CORRUPTION = new Set([23, 661, 25, 112, 398, 400, 163, 32, 636, 24]);
43	const SPREADS_CRIMSON = new Set([199, 662, 203, 234, 399, 401, 200, 352, 205, 201]);
44	const SPREADS_HALLOW = new Set([109, 492, 117, 116, 402, 403, 164, 115, 110, 113]);
45	// Main.tileAlch（Main.cs:7755）
46	const TILE_ALCH = new Set([82, 83, 84]);
47	// Main.tileMoss（= TileID.Sets.Conversion.Moss，TileID.cs:38）
48	const TILE_MOSS = new Set([182, 180, 179, 381, 183, 181, 534, 536, 539, 625, 627]);
49	const TILE_MOSS_BRICK = new Set([512, 513, 514, 515, 516, 517, 535, 537, 540, 626, 628]);
50	// TileID.Sets.Conversion.*（TileID.cs:14-36）
51	const CONV_JUNGLE_GRASS = new Set([60, 661, 662]);
52	const CONV_GRASS = new Set([2, 23, 199, 109, 477, 492]);
53	const CONV_GOLF_GRASS = new Set([477, 492]);
54	const CONV_STONE = new Set([1, 25, 117, 203]);
55	const CONV_ICE = new Set([161, 163, 164, 200]);
56	const CONV_SAND = new Set([53, 112, 116, 234]);
57	const CONV_HARD_SAND = new Set([397, 398, 402, 399]);
58	const CONV_SANDSTONE = new Set([396, 400, 403, 401]);
59	const CONV_THORN = new Set([32, 352, 69, 655]);
60	const CONV_SNOW = new Set([147]);
61	// WallID.Sets.Conversion.*（WallID.cs:11-33）
62	const WALL_CONV_GRASS = new Set([63, 64, 65, 66, 67, 68, 69, 70, 81, 264, 265, 268]);
63	const WALL_CONV_STONE = new Set([1, 61, 185, 3, 28, 83, 262, 274, 246, 248, 269, 349]);
64	const WALL_CONV_HARD_SAND = new Set([216, 217, 219, 218, 304, 305, 307, 306]);
65	// WallID.Sets.Conversion.Dirt（WallID.cs:15）——净化的墙分支（Convert case 11）专用
66	const WALL_CONV_DIRT = new Set([2, 16]);
67	const WALL_CONV_SANDSTONE = new Set([187, 220, 222, 221, 275, 308, 310, 309]);
68	const WALL_CONV_PURE_SAND = new Set([216, 187, 304, 275]);
69	const WALL_CONV_NEW1 = new Set([188, 192, 200, 204, 212, 276, 280, 288, 292, 300]);
70	const WALL_CONV_NEW2 = new Set([189, 193, 201, 205, 213, 277, 281, 289, 293, 301]);
71	const WALL_CONV_NEW3 = new Set([190, 194, 202, 206, 214, 278, 282, 290, 294, 302]);
72	const WALL_CONV_NEW4 = new Set([191, 195, 203, 207, 215, 279, 283, 291, 295, 303]);
73	// WallID.Sets.Spreads*（WallID.cs:48-52）
74	const WALL_SPREADS_CORRUPTION = new Set([69, 217, 220, 3]);
75	const WALL_SPREADS_CRIMSON = new Set([83, 81, 218, 221]);
76	const WALL_SPREADS_HALLOW = new Set([70, 219, 222, 28]);
77	// 雪族（UndergroundTile/OvergroundTile 的钟乳石再生分支）
78	const SNOW_FAMILY = new Set([147, 161, 163, 164, 200]);
79	// 上面所有 sheet 集合 → 内部 id 集合（运行时比较用）
80	const sheetOf = (id: number): number => TILE_DEFS[id]?.vanilla?.sheet ?? -1;
81	// 保底球茎 forceBulb 计分黑名单（WorldGen.cs:74266-74281，sheet=原版 id）：
82	// IsAContainer（BasicChest{21,88,467} 近似）+ 蜥蜴砖/祭坛/门/各结构块/轨道/巨石。
83	// Paintings 近似取 242-244，Boulders 取 379（TileID.Sets 两集合的已知成员）
84	const BURST_BLACKLIST = new Set<number>([
85	  21, 88, 467,            // IsAContainer
86	  226, 237, 10, 11, 26, 16, 15, 14, 104, 158, 105, 531, 349, 137, 443, 12, 314,
87	  242, 243, 244,          // TileID.Sets.Paintings 近似
88	  379,                    // TileID.Sets.Boulders
89	]);
90	
91	export interface EvolutionHost {
92	  store: TileStore;
93	  worldSurface: number;
94	  rockLevel: number;
95	  /** Main.dungeonX（保底灯泡搜索框用，GeneratePlanteraBulbOnAllMechsDefeated cs:74182） */
96	  dungeonX: number;
97	  /** 世界难度 0/1/2（生命果密度门 cs:74514-74528） */
98	  difficulty: number;
99	  flags: Record<string, boolean>;
100	  crimson: boolean;
101	  isDay: boolean;
102	  raining: boolean;
103	  maxRaining: number;
104	  windSpeedCurrent: number;
105	  /** 玩家 tile 坐标（LOS 近似用；null = 无玩家） */
106	  playerTile: () => { x: number; y: number } | null;
107	}
108	
109	export class WorldEvolution {
110	  private hardMode = false;
111	  private allowedToSpread = true;
112	
113	  constructor(private host: EvolutionHost) {}
114	
115	  /** 每 tick 调用（Game.fixedUpdate）。原版 UpdateWorld 采样骨架（cs:71507-71639） */
116	  update(): void {
117	    const st = this.host.store;
118	    const { w, h } = st;
119	    this.hardMode = !!this.host.flags.hardMode;
120	    this.allowedToSpread = true; // CreativePowers.StopBiomeSpread 无对应系统
121	    const rate = 1; // desiredWorldTilesUpdateRate = dayRate = 1（Main.cs:6290；睡觉 ×5 未接）
122	    const wallDist = 3;
123	    // 地表采样（雨天 ×1.5，cs:71552-71557）
124	    let surfaceN = w * h * 3e-5 * rate;
125	    if (this.host.raining) surfaceN *= 1.5;
126	    const num7 = 151 + (151 * 2.8 - 151) * Math.min(1, Math.max(0, w / 4200 - 1));
127	    for (let n = 0; n < surfaceN; n++) {
128	      if (Math.random() * (num7 * 100) < 1) this.plantAlch();
129	      const i = 10 + Math.floor(Math.random() * (w - 20));
130	      const j = 10 + Math.floor(Math.random() * (Math.floor(this.host.worldSurface) - 1 - 10));
131	      if (j < 10) continue;
132	      this.overgroundTile(i, j, wallDist);
133	    }
134	    // 地下采样（cs:71624-71633）
135	    const underN = w * h * 1.5e-5 * rate;
136	    for (let n = 0; n < underN; n++) {
137	      const i = 10 + Math.floor(Math.random() * (w - 20));
138	      const j = Math.floor(this.host.worldSurface) - 1 + Math.floor(Math.random() * (h - 20 - (Math.floor(this.host.worldSurface) - 1)));
139	      if (j < 10 || j >= h - 20) continue;
140	      this.undergroundTile(i, j, wallDist);
141	    }
142	  }
143	
144	  // ================= 通用读写助手 =================
145	
146	  private at(x: number, y: number) { return this.host.store.idx(x, y); }
147	  private active(x: number, y: number): boolean {
148	    const st = this.host.store;
149	    return st.inBounds(x, y) && st.flags[st.idx(x, y)] !== 0;
150	  }
151	  private typeAt(x: number, y: number): number {
152	    const st = this.host.store;
153	    return st.inBounds(x, y) ? st.type[st.idx(x, y)] : -1;
154	  }
155	  private isLava(x: number, y: number): boolean {
156	    const st = this.host.store;
157	    return st.inBounds(x, y) && st.liquid[st.idx(x, y)] > 0 && st.liquidType[st.idx(x, y)] === 2;
158	  }
159	  private liquidAt(x: number, y: number): number {
160	    const st = this.host.store;
161	    return st.inBounds(x, y) ? st.liquid[st.idx(x, y)] : 0;
162	  }
163	  private setType(x: number, y: number, type: number): void {
164	    const st = this.host.store;
165	    if (st.inBounds(x, y)) st.setTile(x, y, type);
166	  }
167	  private kill(x: number, y: number): void { this.setType(x, y, 0); }
168	  /** WorldGen.SolidTile：active && tileSolid && !solidTop && !half && slope==0 */
169	  private solidTile(x: number, y: number): boolean {
170	    const st = this.host.store;
171	    if (!st.inBounds(x, y)) return false;
172	    const i = st.idx(x, y);
173	    if (!st.flags[i]) return false;
174	    const d = TILE_DEFS[st.type[i]];
175	    return !!d && d.solid && !d.platform && !st.half[i] && st.slope[i] === 0;
176	  }
177	  /** PlayerLOS 近似：50 格内有玩家视为可见（原版逐玩家 Collision.CanHitLine 扫描） */
178	  private playerLOS(x: number, y: number): boolean {
179	    const p = this.host.playerTile();
180	    if (!p) return false;
181	    return Math.abs(p.x - x) < 50 && Math.abs(p.y - y) < 40;
182	  }
183	  private mechAny(): boolean {
184	    const f = this.host.flags;
185	    return !!(f['downed_125'] || f['downed_126'] || f['downed_127'] || f['downed_134']);
186	  }
187	  private mechAll(): boolean {
188	    const f = this.host.flags;
189	    // 原版 downedMechBoss1/2/3 = 毁灭者(134)/双子(125)/机械骷髅王(127)
190	    return !!(f['downed_134'] && f['downed_125'] && f['downed_127']);
191	  }
192	
193	  /** 放置 1×1 装饰（帧 style×18,0；目标须为空——PlaceTile EmptyTile 门） */
194	  private place1(x: number, y: number, sheet: number, style = 0, variants = 1): boolean {
195	    const st = this.host.store;
196	    if (!st.inBounds(x, y) || this.active(x, y)) return false;
197	    const t = S(sheet);
198	    if (!t) return false;
199	    st.setTile(x, y, t, (style + (variants > 1 ? Math.floor(Math.random() * variants) : 0)) * 18, 0);
200	    return true;
201	  }
202	  /** 放置 w×h 整件（帧 dx*18, dy*18；区域全空才放）。styleX=样式基偏移（X 轴，
203	   *  如 2×2 横排样式族 style*36，TileObjectData StyleHorizontal=true） */
204	  private placeBlock(x: number, y: number, sheet: number, w: number, h: number, styleX = 0): boolean {
205	    const st = this.host.store;
206	    for (let dx = 0; dx < w; dx++) for (let dy = 0; dy < h; dy++) {
207	      if (!st.inBounds(x + dx, y + dy) || this.active(x + dx, y + dy)) return false;
208	    }
209	    const t = S(sheet);
210	    if (!t) return false;
211	    for (let dx = 0; dx < w; dx++) for (let dy = 0; dy < h; dy++) {
212	      st.setTile(x + dx, y + dy, t, styleX + dx * 18, dy * 18);
213	    }
214	    return true;
215	  }
216	
217	  // ================= 保底世花球茎（GeneratePlanteraBulbOnAllMechsDefeated） =================
218	
219	  /** WorldGen.GeneratePlanteraBulbOnAllMechsDefeated（cs:74180-74248）+ AttemptTo
220	   *  GeneratePlanteraBulbAt（cs:74250-74329）：三王全灭时在丛林侧搜索框
221	   *  [worldSurface, UnderworldLayer) 内掷 2500 点，逐点向上走找丛林草 60
222	   *  （<500 次后 forceBulb 允许泥 59，<200 次后允许非活动格）；找到即试
223	   *  (X,Y-1)/(X-1,Y-1)/(X+1,Y-1) 三锚，**恰好生成一个**灯泡即返回。 */
224	  planteraBulbBurst(): boolean {
225	    const st = this.host.store;
226	    const { w } = st;
227	    const jungleLeft = this.host.dungeonX > w * 0.5;   // 丛林在地牢对侧（cs:74182）
228	    const x0 = Math.floor(jungleLeft ? w * 0.15 : w * 0.65);
229	    const x1 = Math.floor(jungleLeft ? w * 0.35 : w * 0.85);
230	    const yTop = Math.floor(this.host.worldSurface);
231	    const yBot = st.h - 200;                            // Main.UnderworldLayer（Main.cs:2863）
232	    let tries = 2500;
233	    while (tries > 0) {
234	      const forceBulb = tries < 500;                     // cs:74197 允许泥 59+计分档
235	      const allowInactive = tries < 200;                 // cs:74198
236	      tries--;
237	      let px = x0 + Math.floor(Math.random() * (x1 - x0));
238	      let py = yTop + Math.floor(Math.random() * (yBot - yTop));
239	      // 逐点向上走（≤500 步,cs:74206-74230）：flag2 时非活动格也算；命中 60/59 停
240	      let foundSheet = -1;
241	      let steps = 500;
242	      while (steps-- > 0) {
243	        py--;
244	        if (py < yTop) break;
245	        const sheet = sheetOf(this.typeAt(px, py));
246	        const act = this.active(px, py);
247	        if (allowInactive || act) {
248	          if (sheet === 60 || (forceBulb && sheet === 59)) { foundSheet = sheet; break; }
249	        }
250	      }
251	      if (foundSheet < 0) continue;
252	      // 三锚尝试（cs:74232-74246）：PlaceJunglePlant 锚=2×2 右下角 → placeBlock 左上角
253	      for (const ox of [0, -1, 1]) {
254	        if (this.attemptBulbAt(px + ox, py - 1, forceBulb)) return true;
255	      }
256	    }
257	    return false;
258	  }
259	
260	  /** AttemptToGeneratePlanteraBulbAt（cs:74250-74329）。锚 (i,j)=灯泡 2×2 右下角
261	   *  （底行 j 之下两列须为实心丛林草,cs:47820-47827）；2×2 须空或仅藤蔓/蘑菇/
262	   *  丛林植物 233（cs:47795-47812，可覆写）。forceBulb 档：4×4 邻域计分
263	   *  （泥/草 +1,其余活动格 -1,黑名单格直接 -1）≥12 才放,且清 4×4 + 外环转草 60。 */
264	  private attemptBulbAt(i: number, j: number, forceBulb: boolean): boolean {
265	    const st = this.host.store;
266	    // 底承:两列 j+1 行须实心丛林草
267	    if (!(st.inBounds(i - 1, j + 1) && st.inBounds(i, j + 1))) return false;
268	    if (!(this.solidTile(i - 1, j + 1) && sheetOf(this.typeAt(i - 1, j + 1)) === 60)) return false;
269	    if (!(this.solidTile(i, j + 1) && sheetOf(this.typeAt(i, j + 1)) === 60)) return false;
270	    // 2×2 清场校验（藤 62 / 蘑菇植物 63-71 / 丛林植物 233 可覆写）
271	    for (let dx = -1; dx <= 0; dx++) {
272	      for (let dy = -1; dy <= 0; dy++) {
273	        const sh = sheetOf(this.typeAt(i + dx, j + dy));
274	        if (this.active(i + dx, j + dy)
275	          && sh !== 62 && sh !== 233 && !(sh >= 63 && sh <= 71)) return false;
276	      }
277	    }
278	    if (forceBulb) {
279	      // 计分（cs:74255-74296）：4×4 邻域泥/草 +1、其余活动 -1；黑名单即 -1
280	      let score = 0;
281	      outer:
282	      for (let k = -2; k < 2; k++) {
283	        for (let l = -2; l < 2; l++) {
284	          const sh = sheetOf(this.typeAt(i + k, j + l));
285	          if (sh === 59 || sh === 60) score++;
286	          else if (this.active(i + k, j + l)) score--;
287	          if (BURST_BLACKLIST.has(sh)) { score = -1; break outer; }
288	        }
289	      }
290	      if (score < 12) return false;
291	      // 清 4×4 + 外环转丛林草（cs:74298-74309）
292	      for (let m = -2; m < 2; m++) {
293	        for (let n = -2; n < 2; n++) {
294	          this.kill(i + m, j + n);
295	          if (m === -2 || m === 1 || n === -2 || n === 1) this.setType(i + m, j + n, S(60)!);
296	        }
297	      }
298	    }
299	    return this.placeBlock(i - 1, j - 1, 238, 2, 2);
300	  }
301	
302	  // ================= 采样子分发（OvergroundTile cs:72066） =================
303	
304	  private overgroundTile(i: number, j: number, wallDist: number): void {
305	    const st = this.host.store;
306	    const minI = Math.max(10, i - 1), maxI = Math.min(st.w - 10, i + 2);
307	    const minJ = Math.max(10, j - 1), maxJ = Math.min(st.h - 10, j + 2);
308	    if (!st.inBounds(i, j)) return;
309	    const t = this.typeAt(i, j);
310	    const act = this.active(i, j);
311	    // tileAlch 族 → GrowAlch（三态生长/开花/淹死）
312	    if (act && TILE_ALCH.has(sheetOf(t))) { this.growAlch(i, j); return; }
313	    // 海洋沙滩贝壳/珊瑚沉积（cs:72093-72130）：沙滩区上空采样
314	    if (!act && j < this.host.worldSurface + 10) {
315	      // 海岸带判定（oceanDepths 简化：两侧 7% 带）
316	      const beach = 380;
317	      if (i < beach || i > st.w - beach) {
318	        let num5 = 3000 - Math.floor(Math.abs(this.host.windSpeedCurrent) * 1250);
319	        if (this.host.raining) num5 -= Math.floor(1250 * this.host.maxRaining);
320	        if (num5 < 300) num5 = 300;
321	        if (Math.random() * num5 < 1) {
322	          let k = j;
323	          while (k < this.host.worldSurface + 10 && !this.active(i, k) && k - j < 15) k++;
324	          if (this.active(i, k) && sheetOf(this.typeAt(i, k)) === 53) {
325	            k--;
326	            if (this.typeAt(i, k) === S(324)) return;
327	            const need = 2 + Math.floor(Math.random() * 3);
328	            let cnt = 0;
329	            for (let l = i - 10; l <= i + 10; l++) for (let m = k - 10; m <= k + 10; m++) {
330	              const s = sheetOf(this.typeAt(l, m));
331	              if (this.active(l, m) && (s === 324 || s === 81)) cnt++;
332	            }
333	            if (cnt < need) {
334	              if (Math.random() < 0.5 && this.liquidAt(i, k) >= 230) this.place1(i, k, 81, 0, 5);
335	              else this.place1(i, k, 324, 0, 8);
336	            }
337	          }
338	        }
339	      }
340	    }
341	    // 空水格睡莲/香蒲（cs:72237-72248，else-if 互斥各 1/600）：须在下方 !act 早退
342	    // 之前判——原版液体分支（liquid>32）先于 nactive 段，空水格走本支而非刷怪段
343	    if (!act && this.liquidAt(i, j) > 32) {
344	      if (Math.random() < 1 / 600) this.place1(i, j, 518);
345	      else if (Math.random() < 1 / 600) this.place1(i, j, 519);
346	    }
347	    if (!act) {
348	      // 蛛网再生（cs:72534-72536：墙 62 + 无液体 → GrowWeb）
349	      if (st.wall[this.at(i, j)] === 62 && this.liquidAt(i, j) === 0) this.growWeb(i, j);
350	      return; // TrySpawningTownNPC 走独立刷怪系统
351	    }
352	    // hardUpdateWorld（困难模式邪恶/神圣/水晶/叶绿）+ 染料植物
353	    this.hardUpdateWorld(i, j);
354	    if (Math.random() < 1 / 3000) this.plantDye(i, j, false);
355	    else if (this.hardMode && (i < st.w * 0.4 || i > st.w * 0.6) && Math.random() < 1 / 15000) this.plantDye(i, j, true);
356	
357	    const sheet = sheetOf(t);
358	    if (sheet === 80) { // 仙人掌
359	      if (Math.random() < 1 / 15) this.growCactus(i, j);
360	    } else if (CONV_SAND.has(sheet)) {
361	      // 沙族（cs:72275-72309）：海洋贝壳补充 / 内陆仙人掌（绿洲/海藻系统未接）
362	      if (!this.active(i, minJ)) {
363	        const oceanBand = i < 200 || i > st.w - 200;
364	        if (oceanBand) {
365	          if (Math.random() < 1 / 500) {
366	            let cnt = 0;
367	            for (let n = i - 7; n <= i + 7; n++) for (let m = minJ - 7; m <= minJ + 7; m++) {
368	              if (this.active(n, m) && sheetOf(this.typeAt(n, m)) === 81) cnt++;
369	            }
370	            if (cnt < 6 && this.liquidAt(i, minJ) === 255 && this.liquidAt(i, minJ - 1) === 255
371	              && this.liquidAt(i, minJ - 2) === 255 && this.liquidAt(i, minJ - 3) === 255
372	              && this.liquidAt(i, minJ - 4) === 255) {
373	              this.place1(i, minJ, 81, 0, 5);
374	            }
375	          }
376	        } else if (i > 380 + 20 && i < st.w - 380 - 20 && Math.random() < 1 / 300) {
377	          this.growCactus(i, j);
378	        }
379	      }
380	    } else if (SNOW_FAMILY.has(sheet)) {
381	      // 雪原/冰：1/10 悬挂钟乳石（cs:72310-72351 PlaceTight）
382	      if (Math.random() < 1 / 10 && !this.active(i, j + 1) && !this.active(i, j + 2)) {
383	        let cnt = 0;
384	        for (let x = i - 3; x < i + 4; x++) for (let y = j; y <= j + 3; y++) {
385	          if (this.active(x, y) && sheetOf(this.typeAt(x, y)) === 165) cnt++;
386	        }
387	        if (cnt < 2) this.placeTight(i, j + 1);
388	      }
389	    } else if (SPREAD_OVERGROUND.has(sheet)) {
390	      this.grassGrowth(i, j, minI, maxI, minJ, maxJ, false);
391	      if ((sheet === 32 || sheet === 352) && Math.random() < 1 / 3) {
392	        this.growSpike(i, j, sheet, sheet === 32 ? 23 : 199);
393	      }
394	    } else if (TILE_MOSS.has(sheet) || TILE_MOSS_BRICK.has(sheet)) {
395	      this.mossUpdate(i, j, minI, maxI, minJ, maxJ);
396	    } else if (sheet === 3 && Math.random() < 1 / 20) {
397	      // 杂草 → 花（cs:72465-72476；帧 144=蘑菇位除外）
398	      const fx = st.frameX[this.at(i, j)];
399	      if (fx !== 144) this.setType(i, j, S(73));
400	    } else if (sheet === 110 && Math.random() < 1 / 20) {
401	      // 神圣杂草 → 花
402	      const fx = st.frameX[this.at(i, j)];
403	      if (fx < 144) this.setType(i, j, S(113));
404	    }
405	    // 水淹分支（cs:72202-72245）：浸水格——丛林草/神庙砖(60/226) → GrassGrowth；
406	    const liq = this.liquidAt(i, j);
407	    if (liq > 32 && (sheet === 60 || sheet === 226)) this.grassGrowth(i, j, minI, maxI, minJ, maxJ, false);
408	    // 墙蔓延（邪恶草墙/硬化沙墙）
409	    if (this.allowedToSpread) {
410	      this.spreadGrassWalls(wallDist, i, j);
411	      this.spreadDesertWalls(wallDist, i, j);
412	    }
413	    // ---- 藤蔓各族（cs:72534-72852）----
414	    if (!this.active(i, j)) return;
415	    const t2 = this.typeAt(i, j);
416	    const s2 = sheetOf(t2);
417	    // 61 丛林矮草 1/3 → 74 高草（神庙砖上方不长，cs:72591）
418	    if (s2 === 61 && Math.random() < 1 / 3 && sheetOf(this.typeAt(i, j + 1)) !== 226
419	      && st.frameX[this.at(i, j)] < 144) {
420	      if (Math.random() < 1 / 4) st.setTile(i, j, t2, 162 + Math.floor(Math.random() * 8) * 18, st.frameY[this.at(i, j)]);
421	      this.setType(i, j, S(74));
422	      return;
423	    }
424	    // 普通藤（cs:72537-72589）：草 2/藤 52/花藤 382（墙 68/65/66/63 → 花藤）。
425	    // 原版 num24 归一为 1（cs:72551-72554）——挂靠草存在即长
426	    if (s2 === 2 || s2 === 52 || s2 === 382 || (s2 === 192 && Math.random() < 1 / 10)) {
427	      if (this.growMoreVines(i, j) && !this.active(i, j + 1) && !this.isLava(i, j + 1)) {
428	        let vine = 52;
429	        if (s2 !== 52) {
430	          const wl = st.wall[this.at(i, j)], wl2 = st.wall[this.at(i, j + 1)];
431	          if (s2 === 382 || wl === 68 || wl === 65 || wl === 66 || wl === 63 || wl2 === 68 || wl2 === 65 || wl2 === 66 || wl2 === 63) vine = 382;
432	        }
433	        if (this.hasSupport(i, j, 2)) {
434	          this.setType(i, j + 1, S(vine));
435	        }
436	      }
437	      return;
438	    }
439	    this.vineFamily(i, j, s2);
440	  }
441	
442	  /** 各色藤蔓家族通用段（cs:72614-72852 Underground/Overground 同构）：
443	   *  grassSheet=蔓挂靠的草族, vineSheet=要长的藤, vineCap=1/N 概率, vineTopSheet=可续接的顶 */
444	  private vineFamily(i: number, j: number, s2: number): void {
445	    let maxV = 0, anchorSheets: number[] = [], vineSheet = -1;
446	    switch (s2) {
447	      // 丛林藤（60 草/62 藤/384 叶/226 神庙砖）：30；62=10；226=20 且密度门（cs:72602-72649）
448	      case 60: case 384: maxV = 30; anchorSheets = [60, 384, 226]; vineSheet = 62; break;
449	      case 62: maxV = 10; anchorSheets = [60, 384, 226]; vineSheet = 62; break;
450	      case 226:
451	        if (this.tooManyJungleVines(i, j)) return;
452	        maxV = 20; anchorSheets = [60, 384, 226]; vineSheet = 62; break;
453	      // 蘑菇藤（70/528）：70 / 7（cs:72652-72684）
454	      case 70: maxV = 70; anchorSheets = [70]; vineSheet = 528; break;
455	      case 528: maxV = 7; anchorSheets = [70]; vineSheet = 528; break;
456	      // 神圣藤（109/115）：60 / 20（cs:72687-72720）
457	      case 109: maxV = 60; anchorSheets = [109]; vineSheet = 115; break;
458	      case 115: maxV = 20; anchorSheets = [109]; vineSheet = 115; break;
459	      // 腐化藤（23/661/636）：60 / 20（cs:72723-72756）
460	      case 23: case 661: maxV = 60; anchorSheets = [23, 661]; vineSheet = 636; break;
461	      case 636: maxV = 20; anchorSheets = [23, 661]; vineSheet = 636; break;
462	      // 猩红藤（199/662/205）：60 / 20（cs:72759-72792）
463	      case 199: case 662: maxV = 60; anchorSheets = [199, 662]; vineSheet = 205; break;
464	      case 205: maxV = 20; anchorSheets = [199, 662]; vineSheet = 205; break;
465	      // 灰烬藤（633/638）：70 / 7（cs:72795-72831）
466	      case 633: maxV = 70; anchorSheets = [633]; vineSheet = 638; break;
467	      case 638: maxV = 7; anchorSheets = [633]; vineSheet = 638; break;
468	      default: return;
469	    }
470	    if (!this.growMoreVines(i, j)) return;
471	    if (Math.random() >= 1 / maxV) return;
472	    if (this.active(i, j + 1) || this.isLava(i, j + 1)) return;
473	    // 向上 10 格内找挂靠草（bottomSlope 中断；近似：仅判 type）
474	    let ok = false;
475	    for (let y = j; y > j - 10; y--) {
476	      if (!this.host.store.inBounds(i, y)) break;
477	      if (this.active(i, y) && anchorSheets.includes(sheetOf(this.typeAt(i, y)))) { ok = true; break; }
478	    }
479	    if (ok) this.setType(i, j + 1, S(vineSheet));
480	  }
481	
482	  /** 藤顶挂靠检查（普通藤专用：向上找 2 号草，cs:72563-72579） */
483	  private hasSupport(i: number, j: number, grassSheet: number): boolean {
484	    for (let y = j; y > j - 10; y--) {
485	      if (!this.host.store.inBounds(i, y)) break;
486	      if (this.active(i, y) && sheetOf(this.typeAt(i, y)) === grassSheet) return true;
487	    }
488	    return false;
489	  }
490	
491	  // ================= UndergroundTile（cs:73266） =================
492	
493	  private undergroundTile(i: number, j: number, wallDist: number): void {
494	    const st = this.host.store;
495	    if (!st.inBounds(i, j)) return;
496	    const minI = Math.max(10, i - 1), maxI = Math.min(st.w - 10, i + 2);
497	    const minJ = Math.max(10, j - 1), maxJ = Math.min(st.h - 10, j + 2);
498	    const t = this.typeAt(i, j);
499	    if (!this.active(i, j)) return; // 原版地下非活性格无演化（GrowWeb 是地表专属段）
500	    const sheet = sheetOf(t);
501	    if (TILE_ALCH.has(sheet)) { this.growAlch(i, j); return; }
502	    this.hardUpdateWorld(i, j);
503	    if (Math.random() < 1 / 2500) this.plantDye(i, j, false);
504	    else if (this.hardMode && Math.random() < 1 / 10000) this.plantDye(i, j, true);
505	    // ---- 原版 else-if 链（cs:73305-73788）：60 走 GrassGrowth 被消费，
506	    //      丛林藤(62)/蜂巢由既有藤 tile 驱动生长 ----
507	    if (SPREAD_UNDERGROUND.has(sheet)) {
508	      this.grassGrowth(i, j, minI, maxI, minJ, maxJ, true);
509	      if ((sheet === 32 || sheet === 352) && Math.random() < 1 / 3) {
510	        this.growSpike(i, j, sheet, sheet === 32 ? 23 : 199);
511	      }
512	    } else if (sheet === 61 && Math.random() < 1 / 3 && sheetOf(this.typeAt(i, j + 1)) !== 226
513	      && st.frameX[this.at(i, j)] < 144) {
514	      // 61 → 74 高草（cs:73336-73352）
515	      if (Math.random() < 1 / 4) st.setTile(i, j, t, 162 + Math.floor(Math.random() * 8) * 18, st.frameY[this.at(i, j)]);
516	      this.setType(i, j, S(74));
517	    } else if ((sheet === 60 || sheet === 62) && Math.random() < 1 / 5 && this.growMoreVines(i, j)) {
518	      // 丛林藤延长（62 驱动，cs:73355-73393）
519	      if (!this.active(i, j + 1) && !this.isLava(i, j + 1) && this.hasSupportMulti(i, j, [60])) {
520	        this.setType(i, j + 1, S(62));
521	      }
522	    } else if ((sheet === 633 || sheet === 638) && this.growMoreVines(i, j)) {
523	      // 灰烬藤（cs:73403-73430）
524	      const maxV = sheet === 638 ? 7 : 70;
525	      if (Math.random() < 1 / maxV && !this.active(i, j + 1) && !this.isLava(i, j + 1)
526	        && this.hasSupportMulti(i, j, [633])) this.setType(i, j + 1, S(638));
527	    } else if ((sheet === 60 || sheet === 62) && Math.random() < 1 / 80 && !this.playerLOS(i, j)) {
528	      // 蜂巢再生（444 2×2，cs:73541-73605）：62 藤位锚定，主体行 = j（替换藤位，
529	      // 原版 num15 = j for 62）；60 驱动则为 j+1
530	      this.placeJungleBlock2x2(i, j, 444, sheet === 62 ? j : j + 1);
531	    } else if (sheet === 396 && Math.random() < 1 / 80 && !this.playerLOS(i, j)) {
532	      // 蚁狮幼虫（485/稀有 751，cs:73608-73693）：PlaceTile(i, j-1, num24, style)——
533	      // 两 id 均 Style2x2（TileObjectData.cs:3504-3511 / 3997-4011），主体占 j-2..j-1 两行
534	      // （锚 Origin=(0,1) 底行坐沙面）；485 变体 StyleHorizontal+WrapLimit4 →
535	      // frameX=style*36（cs:73687 style=genRand.Next(4)；此前 1×1+style*18 双错）
536	      if (Math.random() < 1 / 100) { this.placeBlock(i, j - 2, 751, 2, 2); }
537	      else {
538	        let clear = true;
539	        for (let d = -1; d >= -3; d--) { if (this.active(i, j + d)) { clear = false; break; } }
540	        if (clear) this.placeBlock(i, j - 2, 485, 2, 2, Math.floor(Math.random() * 4) * 36);
541	      }
542	    } else if (sheet === 69) {
543	      this.growSpike(i, j, 69, 60);
544	    } else if (SNOW_FAMILY.has(sheet)) {
545	      // 冰雪钟乳石再生（cs:73704-73751）
546	      if (Math.random() < 1 / 10 && !this.active(i, j + 1) && !this.active(i, j + 2)) {
547	        let cnt = 0;
548	        for (let x = i - 3; x < i + 4; x++) for (let y = j; y <= j + 3; y++) {
549	          if (this.active(x, y) && sheetOf(this.typeAt(x, y)) === 165) cnt++;
550	        }
551	        if (cnt < 2) this.placeTight(i, j + 1);
552	      }
553	    } else if (TILE_MOSS.has(sheet) || TILE_MOSS_BRICK.has(sheet)) {
554	      this.mossUpdate(i, j, minI, maxI, minJ, maxJ);
555	    } else if (sheet === 661 || sheet === 636 || sheet === 662 || sheet === 205 || sheet === 70 || sheet === 528) {
556	      // 地下藤族延长（cs:73433-73502）：70/528 蘑菇藤、661/636 腐化藤、662/205 猩红藤
557	      if (sheet === 70 || sheet === 528) {
558	        const maxV = sheet === 528 ? 7 : 70;
559	        if (this.growMoreVines(i, j) && Math.random() < 1 / maxV
560	          && !this.active(i, j + 1) && !this.isLava(i, j + 1)
561	          && this.hasSupportMulti(i, j, [70])) this.setType(i, j + 1, S(528));
562	      } else {
563	        const corrupt = sheet === 661 || sheet === 636;
564	        const maxV = (sheet === 636 || sheet === 205) ? 7 : 70;
565	        if (this.growMoreVines(i, j) && Math.random() < 1 / maxV
566	          && !this.active(i, j + 1) && !this.isLava(i, j + 1)
567	          && this.hasSupportMulti(i, j, corrupt ? [661] : [662])) {
568	          this.setType(i, j + 1, S(corrupt ? 636 : 205));
569	        }
570	      }
571	    }
572	  }
573	
574	  private hasSupportMulti(i: number, j: number, sheets: number[]): boolean {
575	    for (let y = j; y > j - 10; y--) {
576	      if (!this.host.store.inBounds(i, y)) break;
577	      if (this.active(i, y) && sheets.includes(sheetOf(this.typeAt(i, y)))) return true;
578	    }
579	    return false;
580	  }
581	
582	  /** 蜂巢 2×2 放置（cs:73550-73605）：锚 2 宽实心+无液，主体区仅 tileCut
583	   *  藤草类可被覆写（原版 KillTile），实心块/岩浆阻断；20 格密度门 */
584	  private placeJungleBlock2x2(i: number, j: number, sheet: number, floorY: number): boolean {
585	    void j;
586	    for (let k = i; k < i + 2; k++) {
587	      if (!this.solidTile(k, floorY - 1)) return false;
588	      if (this.liquidAt(k, floorY - 1) > 0) return false;
589	      for (let l = floorY; l < floorY + 2; l++) {
590	        if (this.isLava(k, l)) return false;
591	        if (this.active(k, l)) {
592	          const s = sheetOf(this.typeAt(k, l));
593	          // tileCut 藤草族可覆写（原版 !tileCut || type==444 阻断条件）
594	          const cuttable = [52, 61, 62, 69, 71, 73, 74, 110, 113, 115, 184, 201, 205, 233, 352, 382, 528, 636, 637, 638].includes(s);
595	          if (!cuttable) return false;
596	        }
597	      }
598	    }
599	    // 20 格内既有 >0 → 不放（CountNearBlocksTypes cap 1）
600	    for (let x = i - 20; x <= i + 20; x += 2) for (let y = floorY - 20; y <= floorY + 20; y += 2) {
601	      if (this.active(x, y) && sheetOf(this.typeAt(x, y)) === sheet) return false;
602	    }
603	    for (let dx = 0; dx < 2; dx++) for (let dy = 0; dy < 2; dy++) {
604	      if (this.active(i + dx, floorY + dy)) this.kill(i + dx, floorY + dy);
605	    }
606	    return this.placeBlock(i, floorY, sheet, 2, 2);
607	  }
608	
609	  // ================= GrassGrowth（cs:74333） =================
610	
611	  private grassGrowth(i: number, j: number, minI: number, maxI: number, minJ: number, maxJ: number, underground: boolean): void {
612	    const st = this.host.store;
613	    if (!st.inBounds(i, j)) return;
614	    const sheet = sheetOf(this.typeAt(i, j));
615	
616	    if (underground) {
617	      // 地下半区（cs:74342-74451）：邪恶草转泥/土 + 顶植物；丛林/蘑菇/灰烬草转泥/灰
618	      let toDirt = -1, toMud = -1, plant = -1, plantChance = 1, self = sheet, evilPair = -1;
619	      switch (sheet) {
620	        case 23: toDirt = 0; toMud = 59; self = 23; evilPair = 661; plant = 24; plantChance = 2; break;
621	        case 199: toDirt = 0; toMud = 59; self = 199; evilPair = 662; plant = 201; plantChance = 2; break;
622	        case 661: toDirt = 59; toMud = 0; self = 661; evilPair = 23; plant = 24; plantChance = 2; break;
623	        case 662: toDirt = 59; toMud = 0; self = 662; evilPair = 199; plant = 201; plantChance = 2; break;
624	        case 60: toDirt = 59; plant = 61; plantChance = 10; break;
625	        case 70: toDirt = 59; plant = 71; plantChance = 10; break;
626	        case 633: toDirt = 57; plant = 637; plantChance = 2; break;
627	        case 226: plant = 61; plantChance = 10; toDirt = -1; break;
628	      }
629	      if (plant !== -1 && !this.active(i, minJ) && Math.random() < 1 / plantChance) {
630	        if (!(sheet === 226 && this.tooManyJunglePlants(i, minJ))) {
631	          this.place1(i, minJ, plant, 0, plant === 61 ? 8 : 1);
632	        }
633	      }
634	      // 丛林草花苞专段（cs:74480-74558 地下第二个 switch 的 case 60;两个【独立 if】
635	      // ——238 检查无论成败继续 236 检查,非 else-if 串行）
636	      if (sheet === 60 && !this.active(i, minJ) && Math.random() < 1 / 25
637	        && this.liquidAt(i, minJ) === 0) {
638	        if (this.hardMode && this.mechAll() && Math.random() < 1 / 60) {
639	          // 世花球茎 238（cs:74486-74512:mechBoss1∧2∧3 全灭 + 1/60 + 150 格内无 238）
640	          let has = false;
641	          for (let x = i - 150; x < i + 150 && !has; x += 2) for (let y = j - 150; y < j + 150; y += 2) {
642	            if (this.active(x, y) && sheetOf(this.typeAt(x, y)) === 238) { has = true; break; }
643	          }
644	          if (!has) this.placeBlock(i, minJ - 1, 238, 2, 2);
645	        }
646	        const expert = (this.host.difficulty ?? 0) >= 1;
647	        const lifeDoor = expert ? 30 : 40;   // expert 1/30 / 其余 1/40（cs:74514）
648	        const lifeRange = expert ? 50 : 60;  // expert 60-10（cs:74524-74528）
649	        if (this.hardMode && this.mechAny() && Math.random() < 1 / lifeDoor) {
650	          // 生命果 236（cs:74514-74538:mechAny + 距离内无 236）
651	          let has = false;
652	          for (let x = i - lifeRange; x < i + lifeRange && !has; x += 2) for (let y = j - lifeRange; y < j + lifeRange; y += 2) {
653	            if (this.active(x, y) && sheetOf(this.typeAt(x, y)) === 236) { has = true; break; }
654	          }
655	          if (!has) this.placeBlock(i, minJ - 1, 236, 2, 2);
656	        }
657	      }
658	      if (toDirt !== -1) {
659	        for (let k = minI; k < maxI; k++) {
660	          for (let l = minJ; l < maxJ; l++) {
661	            if ((i === k && j === l) || !this.active(k, l)) continue;
662	            const s = sheetOf(this.typeAt(k, l));
663	            if (s === toDirt) this.spreadGrass(k, l, toDirt, self);
664	            else if (evilPair !== -1 && s === toMud) this.spreadGrass(k, l, toMud, evilPair);
665	          }
666	        }
667	      }
668	      return;
669	    }
670	
671	    // ---- 地表半区（cs:74566-74928）----
672	    switch (sheet) {
673	      case 2: case 23: case 32: case 109: case 199: case 352: case 477: case 492: case 661: case 662: {
674	        // 顶植物（cs:74599-74626）：2→3(1/12) 23/661→24 199/662→201 109→110（均 1/10）
675	        if (!this.active(i, minJ) && this.liquidAt(i, minJ) === 0) {
676	          let plant = -1;
677	          if (sheet === 2 && Math.random() < 1 / 12) plant = 3;
678	          else if ((sheet === 23 || sheet === 661) && Math.random() < 1 / 10) plant = 24;
679	          else if ((sheet === 199 || sheet === 662) && Math.random() < 1 / 10) plant = 201;
680	          else if (sheet === 109 && Math.random() < 1 / 10) plant = 110;
681	          if (plant !== -1) this.place1(i, minJ, plant, 0, plant === 3 ? 7 : 1);
682	        }
683	        // 类型归一（cs:74629-74657）：32→23 352→199 477→2 492→109；邪恶对 23/661、199/662
684	        let grass = sheet;
685	        let evilPair = -1;
686	        if (sheet === 32) grass = 23;
687	        else if (sheet === 352) grass = 199;
688	        else if (sheet === 477) grass = 2;
689	        else if (sheet === 492) grass = 109;
690	        if (sheet === 23 || sheet === 661) { grass = 23; evilPair = 661; }
691	        if (sheet === 199 || sheet === 662) { grass = 199; evilPair = 662; }
692	        const evilSelf = sheet === 23 || sheet === 199 || sheet === 661 || sheet === 662;
693	        const evilSpread = this.allowedToSpread && evilSelf;
694	        const convGrassSelf = sheet === 109 || sheet === 492;
695	        // 邻域转换（cs:74658-74786）
696	        for (let k = minI; k < maxI; k++) {
697	          for (let l = minJ; l < maxJ; l++) {
698	            if ((i === k && j === l) || !this.active(k, l)) continue;
699	            const s = sheetOf(this.typeAt(k, l));
700	            // A 段：普通草转泥+泥转邪恶对+邪恶草吞并普通草族（cs:74679-74705）
701	            if (s === 0 || (evilPair !== -1 && s === 59)
702	              || (evilSelf && (s === 2 || s === 109 || s === 477 || s === 492))) {
703	              this.spreadGrass(k, l, 0, grass);
704	              if (evilPair !== -1) this.spreadGrass(k, l, 59, evilPair);
705	              if (evilSpread) {
706	                this.spreadGrass(k, l, 2, grass);
707	                this.spreadGrass(k, l, 109, grass);
708	                this.spreadGrass(k, l, 477, grass);
709	                this.spreadGrass(k, l, 492, grass);
710	                if (evilPair !== -1) this.spreadGrass(k, l, 60, evilPair);
711	              }
712	            }
713	            // B 段：神圣草吞并（cs:74707-74761）
714	            if (s === 0 || (convGrassSelf && (s === 2 || s === 477 || s === 23 || s === 199))) {
715	              this.spreadGrass(k, l, 0, grass);
716	              if (sheet === 109) this.spreadGrass(k, l, 2, grass);
717	              if (sheet === 492) this.spreadGrass(k, l, 477, grass);
718	              if (sheet === 109) this.spreadGrass(k, l, 477, 492);
719	              if (convGrassSelf && this.allowedToSpread) {
720	                this.spreadGrass(k, l, 23, 109);
721	                this.spreadGrass(k, l, 199, 109);
722	              }
723	            }
724	          }
725	        }
726	        return;
727	      }
728	      case 70: {
729	        // 蘑菇草（cs:74765-74818）：顶蘑菇草植物 71（1/10）+ 蘑菇树（未接）+ 泥→70
730	        if (!this.active(i, minJ) && Math.random() < 1 / 10) this.place1(i, minJ, 71, 0, 1);
731	        for (let k = minI; k < maxI; k++) for (let l = minJ; l < maxJ; l++) {
732	          if ((i === k && j === l) || !this.active(k, l)) continue;
733	          if (sheetOf(this.typeAt(k, l)) === 59) this.spreadGrass(k, l, 59, 70);
734	        }
735	        return;
736	      }
737	      case 60: {
738	        // 丛林草（cs:74821-74886）：顶 61（1/7）+ 世花球茎 238 + 生命果 236 + 丛林植物 233 + 泥→60
739	        if (!this.active(i, minJ) && Math.random() < 1 / 7) {
740	          this.place1(i, minJ, 61, 0, 8);
741	        } else if (Math.random() < 1 / 500 && (!this.active(i, minJ)
742	          || [61, 74, 69].includes(sheetOf(this.typeAt(i, minJ))))) {
743	          void 0; // 蘑菇树再生（TryGrowingTreeByType(5)）——树系统运行时接口未接
744	        } else if (Math.random() < 1 / 25 && this.liquidAt(i, minJ) === 0) {
745	          // 丛林植物 233（cs:74837-74856 地表段 style rand(8)——花苞 238/生命果 236
746	          // 属【地下半区】case 60（cs:74480-74558,flag 作用域 :74429 证实）,2026-08-13
747	          // 自地表误植段移除——此前地下丛林永不长花苞,世花链只靠首杀保底单株）
748	          this.place1(i, minJ, 233, Math.floor(Math.random() * 8), 1);
749	        }
750	        for (let k = minI; k < maxI; k++) for (let l = minJ; l < maxJ; l++) {
751	          if ((i === k && j === l) || !this.active(k, l)) continue;
752	          if (sheetOf(this.typeAt(k, l)) === 59) this.spreadGrass(k, l, 59, 60);
753	        }
754	        return;
755	      }
756	      case 226: {
757	        // 神庙砖顶 61（1/7，密度门，cs:74880-74899）
758	        if (!this.active(i, minJ) && Math.random() < 1 / 7 && !this.tooManyJunglePlants(i, minJ)) {
759	          this.place1(i, minJ, 61, 0, 8);
760	        }
761	        return;
762	      }
763	      case 633: {
764	        // 灰烬草（cs:74902-74928）：顶 637（1/10）+ 灰烬→633
765	        if (!this.active(i, minJ) && Math.random() < 1 / 10) this.place1(i, minJ, 637, 0, 4);
766	        for (let k = minI; k < maxI; k++) for (let l = minJ; l < maxJ; l++) {
767	          if ((i === k && j === l) || !this.active(k, l)) continue;
768	          if (sheetOf(this.typeAt(k, l)) === 57) this.spreadGrass(k, l, 57, 633);
769	        }
770	        return;
771	      }
772	    }
773	  }
774	
775	  // ================= 运行时 SpreadGrass（cs:75210） =================
776	
777	  private spreadGrass(i: number, j: number, dirt: number, grass: number): void {
778	    const st = this.host.store;
779	    if (!st.inBounds(i, j)) return;
780	    const ti = this.at(i, j);
781	    if (!st.flags[ti]) return;
782	    if (sheetOf(st.type[ti]) !== dirt) return;
783	    // 运行时守卫（cs:75223-75230）：非邪恶草 + (j≥worldSurface 且 dirt==0) → 不转
784	    // （普通草只在 worldSurface 以上蔓延；邪恶草/丛林草/蘑菇草不受限）
785	    const evil = grass === 23 || grass === 661 || grass === 199 || grass === 662;
786	    if (!evil && j >= this.host.worldSurface && dirt === 0) return;
787	    // 3×3 封死不转；邻岩浆恒不转（cs:75242-75263）
788	    let enclosed = true, lavaNear = false;
789	    for (let dx = -1; dx <= 1 && !lavaNear; dx++) {
790	      for (let dy = -1; dy <= 1; dy++) {
791	        const x = i + dx, y = j + dy;
792	        if (!st.inBounds(x, y)) continue;
793	        const k = this.at(x, y);
794	        if (st.liquid[k] > 0 && st.liquidType[k] === 2) { lavaNear = true; break; }
795	        const d = TILE_DEFS[st.type[k]];
796	        if (!st.flags[k] || !d?.solid) enclosed = false;
797	      }
798	    }
799	    if (lavaNear || enclosed) return;
800	    // CanBeClearedDuringGeneration 守卫（NOT_CLEARABLE = 沙岩族等不可转换表）
801	    if (NOT_CLEARABLE.has(st.type[ti])) return;
802	    // 上方向日葵(27)挡邪恶草（cs:75265-75267）
803	    if (evil && sheetOf(this.typeAt(i, j - 1)) === 27) return;
804	    // 草→草转换走 Convert tiles-only（cs:75273-75283）
805	    if (dirt > 0 && CONV_GRASS.has(dirt)) {
806	      if (grass === 23 || grass === 661) this.convert(i, j, 1, true, false);
807	      else if (grass === 199 || grass === 662) this.convert(i, j, 4, true, false);
808	      else if (grass === 109) this.convert(i, j, 2, true, false);
809	      return;
810	    }
811	    this.setType(i, j, S(grass));
812	  }

814	  // ================= Convert（cs:55113，演化路径用 case 1/2/3/4/8/9） =================
815	
816	  convert(i: number, j: number, conversionType: number, tiles = true, walls = true): void {
817	    const st = this.host.store;
818	    if (!st.inBounds(i, j) || (!tiles && !walls)) return;
819	    const ti = this.at(i, j);
820	    const wall = st.wall[ti];
821	    const act = st.flags[ti] !== 0;
822	    if (!act && wall <= 0) return;
823	    const sheet = act ? sheetOf(st.type[ti]) : -1;
824	    const convWall = (to: number) => { if (wall !== 0 && wall !== to) st.setWall(i, j, to); };
825	    const convTile = (to: number) => { if (act && sheet !== to) this.setType(i, j, S(to)); };
826	    switch (conversionType) {
827	      case 4: // 猩红化
828	        if (walls) {
829	          if (WALL_CONV_GRASS.has(wall) && wall !== 81) convWall(81);
830	          else if (WALL_CONV_STONE.has(wall) && wall !== 83) convWall(83);
831	          else if (WALL_CONV_HARD_SAND.has(wall) && wall !== 218) convWall(218);
832	          else if (WALL_CONV_SANDSTONE.has(wall) && wall !== 221) convWall(221);
833	          else if (WALL_CONV_NEW1.has(wall) && wall !== 192) convWall(192);
834	          else if (WALL_CONV_NEW2.has(wall) && wall !== 193) convWall(193);
835	          else if (WALL_CONV_NEW3.has(wall) && wall !== 194) convWall(194);
836	          else if (WALL_CONV_NEW4.has(wall) && wall !== 195) convWall(195);
837	        }
838	        if (tiles) {
839	          if ((TILE_MOSS.has(sheet) || CONV_STONE.has(sheet)) && sheet !== 203) convTile(203);
840	          else if (CONV_JUNGLE_GRASS.has(sheet) && sheet !== 662) convTile(662);
841	          else if (CONV_GRASS.has(sheet) && sheet !== 199) convTile(199);
842	          else if (CONV_ICE.has(sheet) && sheet !== 200) convTile(200);
843	          else if (CONV_SAND.has(sheet) && sheet !== 234) convTile(234);
844	          else if (CONV_HARD_SAND.has(sheet) && sheet !== 399) convTile(399);
845	          else if (CONV_SANDSTONE.has(sheet) && sheet !== 401) convTile(401);
846	          else if (CONV_THORN.has(sheet) && sheet !== 352) convTile(352);
847	        }
848	        break;
849	      case 2: // 神圣化
850	        if (walls) {
851	          if (WALL_CONV_GRASS.has(wall) && wall !== 70) convWall(70);
852	          else if (WALL_CONV_STONE.has(wall) && wall !== 28) convWall(28);
853	          else if (WALL_CONV_HARD_SAND.has(wall) && wall !== 219) convWall(219);
854	          else if (WALL_CONV_SANDSTONE.has(wall) && wall !== 222) convWall(222);
855	          else if (WALL_CONV_NEW1.has(wall) && wall !== 200) convWall(200);
856	          else if (WALL_CONV_NEW2.has(wall) && wall !== 201) convWall(201);
857	          else if (WALL_CONV_NEW3.has(wall) && wall !== 202) convWall(202);
858	          else if (WALL_CONV_NEW4.has(wall) && wall !== 203) convWall(203);
859	        }
860	        if (tiles) {
861	          if ((TILE_MOSS.has(sheet) || CONV_STONE.has(sheet)) && sheet !== 117) convTile(117);
862	          else if (CONV_GOLF_GRASS.has(sheet) && sheet !== 492) convTile(492);
863	          else if (CONV_GRASS.has(sheet) && sheet !== 109 && sheet !== 492) convTile(109);
864	          else if (CONV_ICE.has(sheet) && sheet !== 164) convTile(164);
865	          else if (CONV_SAND.has(sheet) && sheet !== 116) convTile(116);
866	          else if (CONV_HARD_SAND.has(sheet) && sheet !== 402) convTile(402);
867	          else if (CONV_SANDSTONE.has(sheet) && sheet !== 403) convTile(403);
868	          else if (CONV_THORN.has(sheet)) this.kill(i, j);
869	          if (sheet === 59 && ([109].includes(sheetOf(this.typeAt(i - 1, j)))
870	            || [109].includes(sheetOf(this.typeAt(i + 1, j)))
871	            || [109].includes(sheetOf(this.typeAt(i, j - 1)))
872	            || [109].includes(sheetOf(this.typeAt(i, j + 1))))) {
873	            this.setType(i, j, S(0));
874	          }
875	        }
876	        break;
877	      case 1: // 腐化化
878	        if (walls) {
879	          if (WALL_CONV_GRASS.has(wall) && wall !== 69) convWall(69);
880	          else if (WALL_CONV_STONE.has(wall) && wall !== 3) convWall(3);
881	          else if (WALL_CONV_HARD_SAND.has(wall) && wall !== 217) convWall(217);
882	          else if (WALL_CONV_SANDSTONE.has(wall) && wall !== 220) convWall(220);
883	          else if (WALL_CONV_NEW1.has(wall) && wall !== 188) convWall(188);
884	          else if (WALL_CONV_NEW2.has(wall) && wall !== 189) convWall(189);
885	          else if (WALL_CONV_NEW3.has(wall) && wall !== 190) convWall(190);
886	          else if (WALL_CONV_NEW4.has(wall) && wall !== 191) convWall(191);
887	        }
888	        if (tiles) {
889	          if ((TILE_MOSS.has(sheet) || CONV_STONE.has(sheet)) && sheet !== 25) convTile(25);
890	          else if (CONV_JUNGLE_GRASS.has(sheet) && sheet !== 661) convTile(661);
891	          else if (CONV_GRASS.has(sheet) && sheet !== 23) convTile(23);
892	          else if (CONV_ICE.has(sheet) && sheet !== 163) convTile(163);
893	          else if (CONV_SAND.has(sheet) && sheet !== 112) convTile(112);
894	          else if (CONV_HARD_SAND.has(sheet) && sheet !== 398) convTile(398);
895	          else if (CONV_SANDSTONE.has(sheet) && sheet !== 400) convTile(400);
896	          else if (CONV_THORN.has(sheet) && sheet !== 32) convTile(32);
897	        }
898	        break;
899	      case 3: // 蘑菇化（环境转换器蘑菇）
900	        if (walls && (wall === 64 || wall === 67 || wall === 15 || wall === 247)) convWall(80);
901	        if (tiles) {
902	          if (sheet === 60) convTile(70);
903	          else if (CONV_THORN.has(sheet)) this.kill(i, j);
904	        }
905	        break;
906	      case 8: // 叶绿生长（cs:55693-55700：泥/丛林草 → 叶绿矿 211）
907	        if (tiles && (sheet === 59 || sheet === 60)) this.setType(i, j, S(211));
908	        break;
909	      case 9: // 邪恶退化（cs:55712-55749：邪恶草族 → 泥/丛林草）
910	        if (!tiles) break;
911	        if ([23, 199, 661, 662].includes(sheet)) this.setType(i, j, S(60));
912	        else if (sheet === 0) this.setType(i, j, S(59));
913	        else if (sheet === 25 || sheet === 203) this.setType(i, j, S(1));
914	        else if (sheet === 112 || sheet === 234) this.setType(i, j, S(53));
915	        else if (sheet === 398 || sheet === 399) this.setType(i, j, S(397));
916	        else if (sheet === 400 || sheet === 401) this.setType(i, j, S(396));
917	        else if ([24, 32, 201, 205, 352, 636].includes(sheet)) this.kill(i, j);
918	        break;
919	      case 11: { // 净化（cs:55792-55902：净化粉弹体 10 逐格调用——邪恶/神圣族还原原状）
920	        if (walls) {
921	          const fossil = wall === 61 || wall === 185 || wall === 262 || wall === 274;
922	          if (WALL_CONV_STONE.has(wall) && wall !== 349 && !fossil) convWall(349);
923	          else if (wall === 69 || wall === 70 || wall === 81) {
924	            // 腐化/猩红/神圣草墙 → 地表原版草墙族（9/10 → 63，1/10 → 65；地下 64）
925	            convWall(j < this.host.worldSurface ? (Math.random() * 10 < 9 ? 63 : 65) : 64);
926	          } else if (wall === 262) convWall(61);
927	          else if (wall === 274) convWall(185);
928	          else if (WALL_CONV_DIRT.has(wall) && wall !== 2) convWall(2);
929	          else if (WALL_CONV_NEW1.has(wall) && wall !== 212) convWall(212);
930	          else if (WALL_CONV_NEW2.has(wall) && wall !== 213) convWall(213);
931	          else if (WALL_CONV_NEW3.has(wall) && wall !== 214) convWall(214);
932	          else if (WALL_CONV_NEW4.has(wall) && wall !== 215) convWall(215);
933	          else if (WALL_CONV_HARD_SAND.has(wall) && wall !== 216) convWall(216);
934	          else if (WALL_CONV_SANDSTONE.has(wall) && wall !== 187) convWall(187);
935	        }
936	        // 火把净化（type 4 → style 0，Convert_ActuallyConvertTorch）未接——
937	        // 既有 case 1-4 同样不处理火把转换，保持一致（GAP）
938	        if (!tiles) break;
939	        if (CONV_JUNGLE_GRASS.has(sheet) && sheet !== 60) convTile(60);
940	        else if (CONV_GOLF_GRASS.has(sheet) && sheet !== 477) convTile(477);
941	        else if (CONV_GRASS.has(sheet) && sheet !== 2 && sheet !== 477) convTile(2);
942	        else if (CONV_STONE.has(sheet) && sheet !== 1) convTile(1);
943	        else if (CONV_ICE.has(sheet) && sheet !== 161) convTile(161);
944	        else if (CONV_SAND.has(sheet) && sheet !== 53) convTile(53);
945	        else if (CONV_HARD_SAND.has(sheet) && sheet !== 397) convTile(397);
946	        else if (CONV_SANDSTONE.has(sheet) && sheet !== 396) convTile(396);
947	        break;
948	      }
949	    }
950	  }

952	  // ================= hardUpdateWorld（cs:69661，困难模式） =================
953	
954	  private hardUpdateWorld(i: number, j: number): void {
955	    if (!this.hardMode) return;
956	    const st = this.host.store;
957	    if (!st.inBounds(i, j) || !this.active(i, j)) return;
958	    const sheet = sheetOf(this.typeAt(i, j));
959	    // 水晶碎块生长（cs:69668-69709）：珍珠石/珍珠沙/神圣冰/神圣硬化沙岩 族 + 岩层下 + 1/5
960	    if (CAN_GROW_SHARDS.has(sheet) && j > this.host.rockLevel && Math.random() < 1 / 5) {
961	      const dirs: Array<[number, number]> = [[-1, 0], [1, 0], [0, -1], [0, 1]];
962	      const [dx, dy] = dirs[Math.floor(Math.random() * 4)];
963	      if (!this.active(i + dx, j + dy)) {
964	        let cnt = 0;
965	        for (let k = i - 6; k <= i + 6; k++) for (let l = j - 6; l <= j + 6; l++) {
966	          if (this.active(k, l) && sheetOf(this.typeAt(k, l)) === 129) cnt++;
967	        }
968	        if (cnt < 2) {
969	          const rare = Math.random() < 1 / 50;
970	          this.place1(i + dx, j + dy, 129, rare ? 18 + Math.floor(Math.random() * 6) : Math.floor(Math.random() * 18));
971	        }
972	      }
973	    }
974	    // 叶绿生长（cs:69712-69731）：深处丛林草 1/300 → 周围 10 格泥块(59) → 叶绿
975	    //（原版目标严格 ==59,曾误含丛林草 60 直转矿）
976	    if (j > (this.host.worldSurface + this.host.rockLevel) / 2) {
977	      if (sheet === 60 && Math.random() < 1 / 300) {
978	        const x = i + Math.floor(Math.random() * 21) - 10;
979	        const y = j + Math.floor(Math.random() * 21) - 10;
980	        const s2 = sheetOf(this.typeAt(x, y));
981	        if (this.active(x, y) && s2 === 59
982	          && (!this.active(x, y - 1) || ![5, 236, 702, 238].includes(sheetOf(this.typeAt(x, y - 1))))
983	          && this.canChlorophyteGrow(x, y)) {
984	          this.convert(x, y, 8);
985	        }
986	      }
987	      // 叶绿砖/矿扩散退化周围邪恶块（cs:69733-69755：211/346 → 周围 6 格 Convert 9）
988	      if (sheet === 211 || sheet === 346) {
989	        const x = i + Math.floor(Math.random() * 13) - 6;
990	        const y = j + Math.floor(Math.random() * 13) - 6;
991	        if (this.active(x, y)) this.convert(x, y, 9);
992	      }
993	    }
994	    // 世花已死则邪恶不再蔓延（cs:69757-69759）
995	    if ((this.host.flags['downed_262'] && Math.random() < 1 / 2) || !this.allowedToSpread) return;
996	    // 邪恶草蔓延（cs:69761-69806 腐化 / :69808-69895 猩红 / :69897-69955 神圣 同构）
997	    if (SPREADS_CORRUPTION.has(sheet)) this.evilSpread(i, j, 1);
998	    else if (SPREADS_CRIMSON.has(sheet)) this.evilSpread(i, j, 4);
999	    else if (SPREADS_HALLOW.has(sheet)) this.evilSpread(i, j, 2);
1000	  }
1001	
1002	  /** 邪恶/神圣蔓延主体（cs:69761+ 同构三分支合并）：±3 随机点，1/2 链式重试，
1003	   *  向日葵 2 格内不转，叶绿附近防御性退化。
1004	   *  可转换集差异（腐化/猩红 cs:69774-69805 vs 神圣 cs:69919-69955）：神圣不含
1005	   *  丛林草(60)/丛林刺(69)——神圣不吞丛林 */
1006	  private evilSpread(i: number, j: number, convType: number): void {
1007	    const st = this.host.store;
1008	    const ALLOW: Record<number, number[]> = {
1009	      1: [2, 477, 1, 53, 396, 397, 60, 69, 161],
1010	      4: [2, 477, 1, 53, 396, 397, 60, 69, 161],
1011	      2: [2, 477, 1, 53, 396, 397, 161],
1012	    };
1013	    let again = true;
1014	    while (again) {
1015	      again = false;
1016	      const x = i + Math.floor(Math.random() * 7) - 3;
1017	      const y = j + Math.floor(Math.random() * 7) - 3;
1018	      if (!st.inBounds(x, y)) continue;
1019	      // nearbyChlorophyte → 防御退化（cs:69768-69770；ChlorophyteDefense 非 remix 无操作）
1020	      let chlor = 0;
1021	      for (let k = x - 5; k <= x + 5; k++) for (let l = y - 5; l <= y + 5; l++) {
1022	        const s = sheetOf(this.typeAt(k, l));
1023	        if (this.active(k, l) && (s === 211 || s === 346)) chlor++;
1024	      }
1025	      if (chlor >= 3) return;
1026	      // 向日葵 2 格内（CountNearBlocksTypes(x,y,2,cap1,27)）→ 中止本次
1027	      let sunflower = false;
1028	      for (let k = x - 2; k <= x + 2 && !sunflower; k++) for (let l = y - 2; l <= y + 2; l++) {
1029	        if (this.active(k, l) && sheetOf(this.typeAt(k, l)) === 27) { sunflower = true; break; }
1030	      }
1031	      if (sunflower) continue;
1032	      const s = sheetOf(this.typeAt(x, y));
1033	      const convertible = this.active(x, y)
1034	        && (ALLOW[convType].includes(s) || TILE_MOSS.has(s));
1035	      if (convertible) {
1036	        if (Math.random() < 1 / 2) again = true;
1037	        this.convert(x, y, convType, true, false);
1038	      }
1039	    }
1040	  }
1041	
1042	  /** CanChlorophyteGrow（cs:69525-69576）：35 格内 ≤40 且 85 格内 ≤130（岩层上减半） */
1043	  private canChlorophyteGrow(i: number, j: number): boolean {
1044	    let cap1 = 40, cap2 = 130, r1 = 35, r2 = 85;
1045	    if (j < this.host.rockLevel) { cap1 = 20; cap2 = 65; r1 = 52; r2 = 127; }
1046	    const count = (r: number) => {
1047	      let n = 0;
1048	      for (let x = i - r; x < i + r; x++) for (let y = j - r; y < j + r; y++) {
1049	        if (this.active(x, y) && sheetOf(this.typeAt(x, y)) === 211) n++;
1050	      }
1051	      return n;
1052	    };
1053	    return count(r1) <= cap1 && count(r2) <= cap2;
1054	  }
1055	
1056	  // ================= 墙蔓延（cs:74958/75073） =================
1057	
1058	  private spreadGrassWalls(wallDist: number, i: number, j: number): void {
1059	    const st = this.host.store;
1060	    if (!st.inBounds(i, j)) return;
1061	    const wall = st.wall[this.at(i, j)];
1062	    const sheet = this.active(i, j) ? sheetOf(this.typeAt(i, j)) : -1;
1063	    let conv = -1;
1064	    if (WALL_SPREADS_CRIMSON.has(wall) || (sheet >= 0 && SPREADS_CRIMSON.has(sheet))) conv = 4;
1065	    else if (WALL_SPREADS_CORRUPTION.has(wall) || (sheet >= 0 && SPREADS_CORRUPTION.has(sheet))) conv = 1;
1066	    else if (WALL_SPREADS_HALLOW.has(wall) || (sheet >= 0 && SPREADS_HALLOW.has(sheet))) conv = 2;
1067	    if (conv === -1) return;
1068	    const x = i + Math.floor(Math.random() * 5) - 2;
1069	    const y = j + Math.floor(Math.random() * 5) - 2;
1070	    if (!st.inBounds(x, y)) return;
1071	    const tw = st.wall[this.at(x, y)];
1072	    // 腐化/猩红：目标墙须为草墙族 63-68（cs:74975）；神圣：63/65/66/68（cs:75027）
1073	    const grassWall = tw >= 63 && tw <= 68;
1074	    if (conv === 2 ? !(tw === 63 || tw === 65 || tw === 66 || tw === 68) : !grassWall) return;
1075	    // wallDist 范围内有同族邪恶 tile 才转化（cs:74982-75000）
1076	    const spreads = conv === 1 ? SPREADS_CORRUPTION : conv === 4 ? SPREADS_CRIMSON : SPREADS_HALLOW;
1077	    let near = false;
1078	    for (let k = i - wallDist; k <= i + wallDist && !near; k++) for (let l = j - wallDist; l <= j + wallDist; l++) {
1079	      if (this.active(k, l) && spreads.has(sheetOf(this.typeAt(k, l)))) { near = true; break; }
1080	    }
1081	    if (near) this.convert(x, y, conv, false, true);
1082	  }
1083	
1084	  private spreadDesertWalls(wallDist: number, i: number, j: number): void {
1085	    const st = this.host.store;
1086	    if (!st.inBounds(i, j)) return;
1087	    const wall = st.wall[this.at(i, j)];
1088	    const sheet = this.active(i, j) ? sheetOf(this.typeAt(i, j)) : -1;
1089	    // 仅沙岩/硬化沙墙/块（cs:75077-75085）
1090	    const isSandWall = WALL_CONV_SANDSTONE.has(wall) || WALL_CONV_HARD_SAND.has(wall);
1091	    const isSandTile = sheet >= 0 && (CONV_SANDSTONE.has(sheet) || CONV_HARD_SAND.has(sheet));
1092	    if (!isSandWall && !isSandTile) return;
1093	    let conv = -1;
1094	    if (WALL_SPREADS_CORRUPTION.has(wall) || SPREADS_CORRUPTION.has(sheet)) conv = 1;
1095	    else if (WALL_SPREADS_HALLOW.has(wall) || SPREADS_HALLOW.has(sheet)) conv = 2;
1096	    else if (WALL_SPREADS_CRIMSON.has(wall) || SPREADS_CRIMSON.has(sheet)) conv = 4;
1097	    if (conv === -1) return;
1098	    const x = i + Math.floor(Math.random() * 5) - 2;
1099	    const y = j + Math.floor(Math.random() * 5) - 2;
1100	    if (!st.inBounds(x, y)) return;
1101	    // 目标须为纯沙墙族（cs:75112-75120）
1102	    if (!WALL_CONV_PURE_SAND.has(st.wall[this.at(x, y)])) return;
1103	    const spreads = conv === 1 ? SPREADS_CORRUPTION : conv === 4 ? SPREADS_CRIMSON : SPREADS_HALLOW;
1104	    let near = false;
1105	    for (let k = i - wallDist; k <= i + wallDist && !near; k++) for (let l = j - wallDist; l <= j + wallDist; l++) {
1106	      if (this.active(k, l) && spreads.has(sheetOf(this.typeAt(k, l)))) { near = true; break; }
1107	    }
1108	    if (near) this.convert(x, y, conv, false, true);
1109	  }
1110	
1111	  // ================= 苔藓（Overground/UndergroundTile 同段 cs:72400-72462） =================
1112	
1113	  private mossUpdate(i: number, j: number, minI: number, maxI: number, minJ: number, maxJ: number): void {
1114	    if (Math.random() >= 0.5) return;
1115	    const st = this.host.store;
1116	    const self = sheetOf(this.typeAt(i, j));
1117	    for (let k = minI; k < maxI; k++) {
1118	      for (let l = minJ; l < maxJ; l++) {
1119	        if ((i === k && j === l) || !this.active(k, l)) continue;
1120	        const other = sheetOf(this.typeAt(k, l));
1121	        if (other !== 1 && other !== 38) continue;
1122	        const to = this.mossConversion(self, other);
1123	        if (to) {
1124	          this.spreadGrass(k, l, other, to);
1125	        }
1126	      }
1127	    }
1128	    // 藓堆 184（cs:72447-72462）：1/6 随机相邻空格
1129	    if (Math.random() < 1 / 6) {
1130	      const dirs: Array<[number, number]> = [[-1, 0], [1, 0], [0, -1], [0, 1]];
1131	      const [dx, dy] = dirs[Math.floor(Math.random() * 4)];
1132	      if (!this.active(i + dx, j + dy)) this.place1(i + dx, j + dy, 184, 0, 3);
1133	    }
1134	  }
1135	
1136	  /** MossConversion（cs:71469-71494）：苔↔苔砖对照表 */
1137	  private mossConversion(thisType: number, otherType: number): number {
1138	    if (TILE_MOSS_BRICK.has(thisType) && otherType === 38) return thisType;
1139	    if (TILE_MOSS.has(thisType) && otherType === 1) return thisType;
1140	    const pairs: Record<number, number> = {
1141	      182: 515, 515: 182, 180: 513, 513: 180, 381: 517, 517: 381,
1142	      534: 535, 535: 534, 536: 537, 537: 536, 539: 540, 540: 539,
1143	      625: 626, 626: 625, 627: 628, 628: 627, 183: 516, 516: 183, 181: 514, 514: 181,
1144	    };
1145	    return pairs[thisType] ?? 0;
1146	  }
1147	
1148	  // ================= 药草（PlantAlch cs:45910 / GrowAlch cs:45786 / PlaceAlch） =================
1149	
1150	  private plantAlch(): void {
1151	    const st = this.host.store;
1152	    const x = 20 + Math.floor(Math.random() * (st.w - 40));
1153	    // 深度分布（cs:45917-45921）：1/40 深层、9/10 地表下、1/10 全域
1154	    let y: number;
1155	    const r = Math.random();
1156	    if (r < 1 / 40) y = Math.floor((this.host.rockLevel + st.h) / 2) + Math.floor(Math.random() * (st.h - 20 - Math.floor((this.host.rockLevel + st.h) / 2)));
1157	    else if (r < 1 / 40 + 9 / 10) y = Math.floor(this.host.worldSurface) + Math.floor(Math.random() * (st.h - 20 - Math.floor(this.host.worldSurface)));
1158	    else y = 20 + Math.floor(Math.random() * (st.h - 40));
1159	    if (y >= st.h - 20) return;
1160	    // 向下找首个实心
1161	    let k = y;
1162	    while (k < st.h - 20 && !this.active(x, k)) k++;
1163	    if (!this.active(x, k) || this.active(x, k - 1) || this.liquidAt(x, k - 1) !== 0) return;
1164	    // 密度门（cs:45928-45945）：15×15（随世界宽缩放）内药草 <5
1165	    const rad = Math.floor(15 * (st.w / 4200));
1166	    let cnt = 0;
1167	    for (let dx = Math.max(4, x - rad); dx <= Math.min(st.w - 4, x + rad); dx++) {
1168	      for (let dy = Math.max(4, k - rad); dy <= Math.min(st.h - 4, k + rad); dy++) {
1169	        if (this.active(dx, dy) && TILE_ALCH.has(sheetOf(this.typeAt(dx, dy)))) cnt++;
1170	      }
1171	    }
1172	    if (cnt >= 5) return;
1173	    // PlaceSuitableHerbHere（cs:45948-45977）：按脚下方块选药草种
1174	    const ground = sheetOf(this.typeAt(x, k));
1175	    let style = -1;
1176	    if (ground === 2 || ground === 109) style = 0;
1177	    else if (ground === 60) style = 1;
1178	    else if (ground === 0 || ground === 59) style = 2;
1179	    else if ([23, 661, 25, 203, 199, 662].includes(ground)) style = 3;
1180	    else if ((ground === 53 || ground === 116) && x >= 380 && x <= st.w - 380) style = 4;
1181	    else if (ground === 57 || ground === 633) style = 5;
1182	    else if (SNOW_FAMILY.has(ground)) style = 6;
1183	    if (style === -1) return;
1184	    // PlaceAlch：82 号芽，frameX = style*18
1185	    this.place1(x, k - 1, 82, style);
1186	  }
1187	
1188	  private growAlch(x: number, y: number): void {
1189	    const st = this.host.store;
1190	    if (!this.active(x, y)) return;
1191	    const i = this.at(x, y);
1192	    const fx = st.frameX[i];
1193	    const herb = Math.floor(fx / 18); // 药草种（0-6）
1194	    // 淹死规则（cs:45796-45807）：岩浆除火冠花(5)、水除月光花(1)/水叶(4) 外死亡
1195	    if (this.liquidAt(x, y) > 0) {
1196	      const isLava = st.liquidType[i] === 2;
1197	      const isWater = st.liquidType[i] === 1;
1198	      if (!((isLava && herb === 5) || (isWater && (herb === 1 || herb === 4)))) {
1199	        this.kill(x, y);
1200	        return;
1201	      }
1202	    }
1203	    const sheet = sheetOf(st.type[i]);
1204	    if (sheet === 82) {
1205	      // 芽 → 成熟（cs:45809-45867）：开花条件各自满足时 1/50 直跳 84
1206	      const bloom = (herb === 0 && this.host.isDay) || (herb === 1 && !this.host.isDay)
1207	        || (herb === 2) || (herb === 3) || (herb === 4 && this.host.raining)
1208	        || (herb === 5) || (herb === 6 && y > this.host.worldSurface);
1209	      if (bloom && Math.random() < 1 / 50) {
1210	        // 帧内已有花位的直接成花（帧 0/18/72/36/90 各条件段）；否则走 83
1211	        st.setTile(x, y, S(84), fx, 0);
1212	      } else if (Math.random() < 1 / 50) {
1213	        st.setTile(x, y, S(83), fx, 0);
1214	      }
1215	      return;
1216	    }
1217	    if (sheet === 83) {
1218	      // 成熟 ⇌ 开花（cs:45870-45897）：帧 36 的 1/3 权重摆动
1219	      if (fx === 36 && Math.random() < 2 / 3) {
1220	        if (Math.random() < 1 / 2) st.setTile(x, y, S(84), fx, 0);
1221	      } else if (fx === 108 && Math.random() < 1 / 30) {
1222	        st.setTile(x, y, S(84), fx, 0);
1223	      } else {
1224	        // 满足开花条件 → 1/50 转 84（cs:45809 同构，成熟态沿用）
1225	        const herb2 = herb;
1226	        const bloom = (herb2 === 0 && this.host.isDay) || (herb2 === 1 && !this.host.isDay)
1227	          || (herb2 === 4 && this.host.raining) || (herb2 === 6 && y > this.host.worldSurface);
1228	        if (bloom && Math.random() < 1 / 50) st.setTile(x, y, S(84), fx, 0);
1229	      }
1230	      return;
1231	    }
1232	    if (sheet === 84 && fx === 36 && Math.random() < 1 / 2) {
1233	      // 开花 → 成熟回落（cs:45874-45885 else-if 链）
1234	      st.setTile(x, y, S(83), fx, 0);
1235	    }
1236	  }
```
